### [58] [Bounded-Memory Parallel Image Pulling for Large Container Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05596)
*Sri Saran Balaji Vellore Rajakumar,Henry Wang,Ankur Singh,James Thompson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DBPP方法，通过将容器镜像分块直接写入磁盘目标位置，解决大尺寸AI/ML容器镜像拉取时的内存溢出问题，将峰值内存降低8.7-25.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML工作负载通常以容器形式运行，镜像拉取成为启动时间的关键瓶颈。随着AI/ML镜像压缩后达到31-48GB，现有容器运行时(containerd)的内存有序重组机制会导致内存占用过大，在GPU节点上引发OOM终止。

Method: DBPP(Disk-Backed Parallel Pull)采用磁盘支持的并行拉取策略：将每个数据块直接写入磁盘的目标字节偏移位置，消除顺序依赖；每个层级作为完整的可寻址文件落地磁盘后，同时进行SHA-256摘要验证和解压缩，而containerd必须顺序执行这两个步骤。

Result: 在5个生产级镜像(最大48.5GB)的受控实验中，DBPP将守护进程峰值内存降低8.7-25.3倍，同时保持相当的拉取吞吐量。在内存受限节点上，containerd 2.2拉取31.4GB镜像时被OOM终止，而DBPP成功完成相同操作。

Conclusion: 核心思想具有普适性：任何仅为保证顺序而在内存中缓冲数据的流水线，只要底层存储足够快，都可以将缓冲区移至磁盘，用充足的资源替代稀缺且竞争的资源。该方法不仅适用于容器镜像，还可推广到其他类似场景。

Abstract: AI/ML workloads increasingly run as containers, where a container image must be downloaded to the host before the workload can start. This cold image pull lands on the critical path whenever a training or inference job scales up or a host is updated, and for GPU workloads it has become the dominant component of startup time as AI/ML images reach 31--48~GiB compressed. We present Disk-Backed Parallel Pull (DBPP), an alternative to the in-memory ordered reassembly used by containerd~2.2, the upstream container runtime. containerd splits layers into chunks fetched concurrently over HTTP range requests, but chunks that arrive out of order accumulate in the runtime heap until a sequential consumer drains them in order. This backlog grows with image size, and on GPU nodes where host memory is shared with frameworks and model weights, it leads to out of memory (OOM) termination of the runtime itself. DBPP writes each chunk directly to its target byte offset on disk, eliminating the ordering dependency and bounding memory regardless of image size. Because each layer lands on disk as a complete, seekable file, DBPP runs SHA-256 digest verification and decompression simultaneously, two passes containerd must run one after the other. In controlled experiments across five production-scale images (up to 48.5~GiB), DBPP reduces peak daemon memory by 8.7--25.3$\times$ while maintaining comparable pull throughput. On a memory-constrained node, containerd~2.2 is OOM-killed pulling a 31.4~GiB image while DBPP completes the same pull. The underlying idea reaches past container images: any pipeline that buffers data in memory only to enforce ordering can move that buffer to disk once the backing store is fast enough, trading a scarce, contended resource for an abundant one.

</details>


### [59] [Layer 2 Coordinated Trusted Setup for Continuous CRS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05776)
*Khalid Hassan,Sara Rouhani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种L2协调框架，实现了自动化的连续CRS生成，成功解决了零知识证明系统中信任设置的问题，在保证L2交易吞吐量的同时实现了可靠的连续信任设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识证明系统依赖信任设置阶段生成通用参考字符串（CRS），但现有方法通常是静态、一次性仪式，缺乏灵活性且容易受到长期威胁。将连续、重复的信任设置转移到去中心化L2网络时，高吞吐量交易处理与信任设置仪式的多轮需求之间存在协调挑战。

Method: 设计了两种协议变体：链上智能合约方法和异步点对点共识变体，基于去中心化PBFT协调的ZK-rollup架构。两种设计都利用非交互式零知识知识证明和提交-揭示结构，安全地将交易管道与仪式执行解耦，消除自适应操纵向量并隔离仪式延迟。

Result: 实验评估表明，该架构成功隔离了仪式活性。连续设置在模拟广域网约束和对抗条件下，尽管存在节点退出或恶意贡献，仍能在实际时间范围内可靠完成，同时保持稳定的L2交易吞吐量。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无需中心化协调的自动化连续CRS生成，为零知识证明系统提供了更安全、灵活的信任设置解决方案，在保持系统性能的同时增强了安全性。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proof systems rely on a trusted setup phase to generate a Common Reference String (CRS), yet existing approaches are typically static, one-time ceremonies that are inflexible and vulnerable to long-term compromise. Offloading continuous, recurring trusted setups to a decentralized Layer 2 (L2) network introduces a fundamental coordination challenge arising from the mismatch between high-throughput transaction processing and the multi-round requirements of trusted setup ceremonies. This paper presents an L2-coordinated framework that safely decouples transaction pipelines from ceremony execution to achieve automated, continuous CRS generation without centralized coordination. We design and implement two protocol variants over a decentralized, PBFT-coordinated ZK-rollup architecture: an on-chain smart contract approach and an asynchronous peer-to-peer consensus variant. Both designs utilize non-interactive zero-knowledge proofs of knowledge alongside commit-reveal structures to eliminate adaptive manipulation vectors and isolate ceremony latency. Experimental evaluations under simulated wide-area network constraints and adversarial conditions demonstrate that our architecture successfully isolates ceremony liveness. Continuous setups complete reliably within practical time bounds despite node dropouts or malicious contributions, while preserving stable L2 transaction throughput.

</details>


### [60] [MatrixFSDP: communication-free matrix optimizers under ZeRO-3 parameter sharding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05895)
*Ming Gao,Yanwu Xu,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MatrixFSDP是一种新型分布式训练系统，通过改变ZeRO-3分片布局实现矩阵优化器Muon与FSDP的高效集成，在保持ZeRO-3级别内存效率的同时，显著降低了优化器步骤延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵优化器（如Muon）在大规模训练中具有收敛性和token效率优势，但其需要完整2D矩阵输入的特性与FSDP/ZeRO-3的分片机制存在系统不匹配：现有方案要么每步重建矩阵产生高昂通信开销，要么使用ZeRO-1导致内存占用过大。

Method: 提出MatrixFSDP，采用第三种路径：改变ZeRO-3分片位置而非优化器计算方式。对每个2D权重，让一个数据并行rank持有完整矩阵，其他rank持有空分片；非矩阵张量打包到尾部owner并使用AdamW。引入MatrixShard元数据、负载感知owner规划器、确定性owner-segment P2P集合通信、owner缓冲区固定和owner-shard检查点重分片等技术实现高效正确的非均衡布局。

Result: 在64块A100上，MatrixFSDP相比标准FSDP2-Muon将优化器步骤延迟降低4.2倍（单节点）和54.6倍（八节点），端到端加速达2.15倍，能够运行ZeRO-1 owner placement超出80GB GPU容量的模型规模，同时保持ZeRO-3级别的内存效率。

Conclusion: MatrixFSDP成功解决了矩阵优化器与分布式训练框架的系统不匹配问题，在保持内存效率的同时实现了显著的性能提升，为大规模训练中的矩阵优化器应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Matrix optimizers such as Muon are attractive for large-scale training because they can improve convergence and token efficiency over coordinate-wise optimizers. Muon does this by orthogonalizing momentum-smoothed matrix updates with Newton-Schulz, producing spectrum-balanced updates that require the complete 2D matrix as input. This exposes a systems mismatch: FSDP/ZeRO-3 saves memory by making the optimizer see shards, not whole matrices. Existing systems therefore either reconstruct matrices at every optimizer step, paying weight-sized communication after backward, or make the update local by using ZeRO-1 owner placement with full parameters resident. MatrixFSDP takes a third path: it changes where ZeRO-3 shards live, not the optimizer being computed. For each 2D weight, one data-parallel rank owns the whole matrix and the other ranks hold empty shards; non-matrix tensors are packed into tail owners and stay on AdamW. The ordinary backward reduction then lands the full Muon input on the owner, so Newton-Schulz runs locally with no optimizer-step matrix collective. Forward and backward still materialize and reshard parameters; the runtime challenge is to make that uneven layout efficient and correct. MatrixFSDP does so with MatrixShard metadata, a balance-aware owner planner, deterministic owner-segment P2P collectives, owner-buffer pinning, and owner-shard checkpoint resharding. The resulting update matches full-matrix Muon while preserving ZeRO-3-scale memory: on 64 A100s, MatrixFSDP reduces optimizer-step latency over stock FSDP2-Muon by 4.2x on one node and 54.6x on eight nodes, reaches up to 2.15x end-to-end speedup, and runs model sizes where ZeRO-1 owner placement exceeds an 80 GB GPU.

</details>


### [61] [The Surplus Parking Gathering Problem in Infinite Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05983)
*Animesh Maiti,Abhinav Chakraborty,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Surplus Parking Gathering Problem (SPG)，即在无限网格上让机器人按容量饱和停车点并将剩余机器人聚集的问题，给出了可解性条件、确定性算法及其复杂度上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在机器人数量超过停车容量时，如何协调自主、匿名、无记忆、同质机器人实现停车点饱和与剩余机器人聚集的分布式问题。

Method: 在异步模型下，首先刻画不可解初始配置，然后针对可解配置提出多阶段确定性分布式算法，采用全局视野和强多重性检测避免碰撞。

Result: 算法在有限时间内终止，所有停车点按指定容量饱和，剩余机器人聚集于唯一确定节点；移动复杂度上界为O(n(a+b)+n²)，下界为Ω(n(a+b))。

Conclusion: SPG问题在满足必要条件时可解，所提算法正确且高效，为机器人协调问题提供了新的理论框架与复杂度分析。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the \emph{Surplus Parking Gathering Problem} ($\mathcal{SPG}$), a new coordination problem for robots deployed on an infinite grid. The input consists of a set of designated parking nodes, each associated with a prescribed capacity, while the total number of robots exceeds the total parking capacity. The objective is to saturate every parking node exactly according to its capacity while gathering all remaining surplus robots at a common grid node that is not specified a priori. The robots are assumed to be autonomous, anonymous, oblivious, identical, disoriented, and homogeneous. We consider the asynchronous (\textsc{async}) model with global visibility and global strong multiplicity detection.
  We first establish necessary conditions for the solvability of $\mathcal{SPG}$ by characterizing the initial configurations that admit no deterministic distributed algorithm. For all the remaining solvable configurations, we present a deterministic distributed algorithm that correctly solves the problem. The proposed algorithm proceeds in several phases and avoids collisions throughout its execution. We prove that the algorithm terminates in finite time and, upon termination, every parking node is saturated according to its prescribed capacity while all surplus robots are gathered at a uniquely determined gathering node. We further analyze the move complexity of the proposed algorithm, obtaining an $O(n(a+b)+n^2)$ upper bound together with an $Ω(n(a+b))$ worst-case lower bound for the $\mathcal{SPG}$ problem.

</details>


### [62] [A Dual-CRDT Architecture for Decentralized Trust Governance and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06068)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双CRDT架构，包含信任CRDT和数据CRDT，将治理信息作为复制状态进行管理，实现了在拜占庭容错环境下的去中心化信任治理和演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CRDT系统在拜占庭容错部署中需要决定信任哪些更新，但信任关系会随时间演化（参与者加入/离开、身份变更、治理规则修订），而这些信任决策信息通常在复制状态之外管理，缺乏统一的治理机制。

Method: 提出双CRDT架构：信任CRDT存储和演化治理信息，数据CRDT根据信任CRDT派生的信任配置进行重构。通过确定性重构和拜占庭信任过滤，使治理成为复制状态而非外部管理的工件，实现递归治理模型。

Result: 构建了一个递归治理模型，治理规则既能决定自身未来演化，同时又能管理应用数据。在Melda和melda-sec之上实现了原型系统，验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该架构为拜占庭容错CRDT系统提供了去中心化信任治理和演化的初步探索，实现了治理与应用数据的统一管理框架。

Abstract: While CRDTs provide decentralized replication and eventual consistency, Byzantine-resilient deployments require mechanisms for deciding which updates should be trusted and therefore contribute to the reconstructed state. In practice, the trust relationships underlying these decisions may evolve over time as participants join or leave, identities change, and governance rules are revised. However, the information used to make trust decisions is typically managed outside the replicated state itself. This paper introduces a dual-CRDT architecture composed of a \emph{Trust CRDT} and a \emph{Data CRDT}. The Trust CRDT stores and evolves governance information, while the Data CRDT is reconstructed according to the trust configuration derived from the Trust CRDT. Governance therefore becomes replicated state rather than an externally managed artifact. Building upon deterministic reconstruction and Byzantine trust filtering, the proposed model allows trust relationships and governance rules to evolve through ordinary CRDT updates. The resulting architecture provides a recursive governance model in which governance rules determine their own future evolution while simultaneously governing application data. The approach is implemented as a prototype on top of Melda and melda-sec and should be viewed as an initial exploration of decentralized trust governance and evolution for Byzantine-resilient CRDT systems.

</details>


### [63] [DDB: Source-Level Interactive Debugging for Distributed Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06107)
*Yibo Yan,Junzhou He,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DDB将交互式调试扩展到分布式系统，通过分布式回溯、意图保持控制平面和时间虚拟化技术，解决了跨进程调用栈、基础设施动态性和暂停触发超时级联等核心难题，实现了安全高效的分布式应用调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式调试器仅适用于单进程执行，在分布式系统中面临三大挑战：调用栈在进程边界中断、调试状态无法在动态基础设施中保持、以及调试器导致的暂停会触发毁灭性的超时级联。开发者被迫放弃实时假设验证，转而采用笨重的日志-重部署迭代循环。

Method: DDB提出三项关键技术：(1) Distributed Backtrace (DBT)在每个RPC中嵌入紧凑的因果元数据，重建跨RPC边界的统一调用栈；(2) 意图保持控制平面在动态进程集中自动协调和传播断点；(3) Pause-Erased Time (PET)虚拟化进程时钟，解耦逻辑时间与物理暂停，防止超时级联。

Result: 在gRPC、ServiceWeaver、Nu和Quicksand上评估（最多122个进程），DDB实现了30ms中位跨RPC回溯延迟、重复执行暂停下<5ms时间跳跃、1-5%吞吐量开销（与单进程调试器相当）。控制用户研究中，DDB实现100%故障定位成功率（基线工具38.5%），中位定位时间约8分钟。

Conclusion: DDB证明了通过针对性技术方案可以将交互式调试有效扩展到分布式应用，在保持低开销的同时大幅提升故障定位效率和成功率，为分布式系统调试提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: Interactive debugging is an effective tool for understanding program behavior at the source level, allowing developers to pause execution, navigate the call stack, and inspect runtime state. However, interactive debuggers are designed for single-process execution, and interactive debugging has been widely considered impractical for distributed systems. Call stacks stop at process boundaries, debugging state fails to survive infrastructure dynamics, and, most critically, debugger-induced execution pauses trigger catastrophic timeout cascades that destroy the intended debug flow. Consequently, developers are forced to abandon live hypothesis testing in favor of unwieldy and iterative log-and-redeploy cycles.
  We present DDB, a source-level interactive debugger that extends interactive debugging capabilities to distributed applications. We show that each of these challenges admits a targeted solution. To bridge disjoint processes, Distributed Backtrace (DBT) embeds compact causality metadata in every RPC and reconstructs a unified call stack across RPC boundaries. To manage the lifecycle of a distributed session, an intent-preserving control plane automatically coordinates and propagates breakpoints across dynamic process sets. To make pausing safe, Pause-Erased Time (PET) virtualizes each process's clock, decoupling logical time from physical pauses and preventing timeout cascades. DDB integrates with an RPC framework in 20-60 lines of code. Evaluated on gRPC, ServiceWeaver, Nu, and Quicksand across up to 122 processes, DDB achieves 30ms median cross-RPC backtrace latency, sub-5 ms time jump under repeated execution pauses, and adds 1-5% throughput overhead, comparable to attaching a single-process debugger. In a controlled user study, DDB achieves a 100% fault localization success rate (compared to 38.5% for baseline tools) with a median localization time of ~8 minutes.

</details>


### [64] [Ordering by Unanimity: Giving Applications Sequencing Rights Without Breaking Composability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06144)
*Andrea Canidio*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出“一致性优先覆盖”算法解决区块链应用对交易执行顺序的偏好问题，提供两大核心保障以确保特定交易按应用偏好顺序执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链应用对交易执行顺序有明确偏好（如AMM使用外部价格预言机时需先更新价格再执行交易；交易所需先执行限价单取消再处理市价单；拍卖应用需按竞价从高到低执行），但底层区块链的交易顺序由系统决定，可能与特定应用的执行顺序要求不兼容。

Method: 引入“一致性优先覆盖”算法，核心思想是：当所有应用对两笔交易的执行顺序达成一致时，底层区块链应遵循该共识；其余情况采用默认顺序（即区块中交易的出现顺序）。算法需解决应用一致性可能形成的循环问题，通过循环打破机制处理。虽然循环打破存在被攻击者制造循环的漏洞，但算法设计了两层保障。

Result: 算法提供两大核心保障：1）所有与单个具有执行偏好的应用交互的交易，即使同时与无偏好的其他应用交互，也按该应用的偏好顺序执行；2）“受保护交易”（在一致性顺序中无法被攻击者构造的交易超越的交易）始终按所有应用一致偏好的顺序执行，即使涉及多个应用。

Conclusion: 两大保障明确了协议能够保护的执行偏好范围，使得应用和发送方能够预先判断哪些交易将按预期顺序执行，为区块链应用提供了可预测的交易排序机制。

Abstract: Blockchain applications may have preferences over the order in which transactions execute: an automated market maker may use an external feed to price its liquidity, and require that the oracle update incorporating this price execute before any swap; an exchange may want to execute cancellations of limit orders before incoming market orders; an application may run an on-chain auction by executing bids from highest to lowest, so that the first bid wins. However, the ordering of transactions is chosen by the underlying blockchain and may not be compatible with the requirements of a specific application. In this paper, I tackle this problem by introducing an algorithm called unanimity override. The intuition is that when all the applications agree on how to order two transactions, the underlying blockchain should respect this agreement; a default order - the order in which transactions appear in the block - settles the rest. The problem with this naive approach is that application unanimity may form cycles, which the algorithm must break. Cycle-breaking is also the rule's main vulnerability because an attacker can insert transactions to manufacture a cycle. Yet two guarantees hold against any attacker who sets the default order, deploys applications, and inserts transactions. All transactions that interact with a single application that expressed preferences are ordered according to that application's preferences, even when they also interact with other applications that did not express preferences. Also, gated transactions - those that cannot be outranked in the unanimity order by any transaction crafted by an attacker - always execute as the applications unanimously prefer, even when they touch many applications. The two guarantees identify the preferences the protocol can protect, and they tell applications and senders in advance which transactions will execute in the intended order.

</details>


### [65] [UBEP: Re-architecting Expert Parallelism Communication Library for Production Superpods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06202)
*Yipeng Liu,Chang Liu,Si Shen,Jiaqi Zheng,Mingfan Li,Yuyang Yang,Guanhua Li,Yuquan Zhang,Yimeng Xu,Zhongzhe Hu,Zhiyuan Huang,Qihang Duan,Junsong Wang,Wenkai Ling,Baochuan Yang,Xianzhi Yu,Han Bao,Yijie Chen,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了UBEP通信库，通过优化All-to-All原语解决了MoE模型在超算集群上部署的三大瓶颈，将All-to-All延迟降低52.4%，推理TPOT降低11.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NVIDIA NVL72/576和华为CloudMatrix384等高带宽超算集群上部署Mixture-of-Experts模型时，尽管系统提供了统一的全局地址空间和高带宽互连，但稀疏MoE通信仍面临三大瓶颈：(1)粗粒度BSP并行导致的严格执行序列化；(2)随互连带宽增长而无法扩展的高昂同步开销；(3)距离无关调度导致的不规则token流量负载不均衡。

Method: 提出UBEP（统一总线专家并行）通信库，针对现代超算集群架构重新设计MoE的All-to-All原语，消除传统通信模式中的序列化、同步和负载均衡瓶颈。

Result: 在大规模实验中，UBEP将All-to-All延迟降低高达52.4%，MoE推理的每输出token时间（TPOT）降低高达11.1%。

Conclusion: UBEP作为生产级通信库，有效释放了现代超算集群在MoE稀疏通信方面的潜力，为大规模MoE模型部署提供了高效的通信解决方案。

Abstract: The deployment of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models on production high-bandwidth superpods, such as NVIDIA's NVL72/576 and Huawei's CloudMatrix384, introduces critical challenges beyond raw interconnect bandwidth. While these systems provide unified global address spaces and high-bandwidth fabrics, their full potential for sparse MoE communication is hindered by three fundamental bottlenecks: (1) Strict execution serialization imposed by coarse-grained Bulk Synchronous Parallel (BSP) orchestration of interdependent communication phases; (2) Prohibitive synchronization overhead that fails to scale alongside high interconnect bandwidth; and (3) Severe load imbalance resulting from distance-agnostic scheduling of irregular token traffic. To eliminate these bottlenecks, we introduce UBEP (Unified-Bus Expert Parallelism), a production-ready communication library that rethinks MoE's All-to-All primitives for modern superpod architectures. Through large scale experiments, UBEP reduces All-to-All latency by up to 52.4% and MoE inference Time Per Output Token (TPOT) by up to 11.1%.

</details>

### [66] [BitFair: A 12nm Bit-Serial CNN Accelerator with Learnable Early Termination and Adaptive Bit Ordering for Ultra-Low-Power XR Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05445)
*Ang Li,Chang Gao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BitFair是一种软硬件协同设计的位串行CNN加速器，通过可学习的位级早退机制和自适应位排序，在XR设备的超低功耗和严格延迟约束下实现高效的神经网络推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR可穿戴设备需要在极低功耗（数瓦）和严格延迟（亚毫秒级）约束下进行神经网络推理。现有的位串行计算架构存在冗余计算问题：即使最终ReLU激活输出为零，仍会处理所有位，造成能量浪费。

Method: 提出BitFair加速器，采用两个核心机制：1）动态位级稀疏性利用，通过学习逐层阈值触发早退——当部分和可靠地预测最终ReLU输出为零时提前终止计算；2）逐层位序优化搜索，优先处理信息量高的位，在不损失精度的前提下最大化早退机会。

Result: 在GlobalFoundries 12nm FinFET工艺下实现，核心面积0.34mm²，片上存储104KB，电压0.55-0.70V。延迟低于1ms，能效达117.0 BTOPS/W，能耗0.07 pJ/SOP。在IBM DVS128手势和N-MNIST数据集上分别达到96.5%和97.7%准确率。相比先前XR视觉加速器，有效能效提升4.0-22.1倍，精度最高提升9.2%。

Conclusion: BitFair成功解决了XR设备中神经网络推理的功耗与延迟难题，通过位级早退和自适应位排序显著提升能效和精度，满足XR应用的苛刻要求，为XR可穿戴设备的高效感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) wearables require always-on perception within tight power envelopes of a few watts and motion-to-photon latency budgets below 20 ms, leaving only a few milliseconds for neural-network inference. Bit-serial computing is attractive for such energy-efficient neural network acceleration, but many existing architectures still process all bits even when ReLU sets the final output to zero. This paper presents BitFair, a software-hardware co-designed bit-serial CNN accelerator with learnable bit-level early termination and adaptive bit ordering, working under the ultra-low-power and strict latency requirements of XR applications. BitFair exploits dynamic bit-level sparsity by learning per-layer thresholds that trigger early termination when partial sums reliably predict that the final ReLU output will be zero. Furthermore, it searches for layer-wise bit orders that prioritize informative bits, maximizing early termination without sacrificing accuracy. A GlobalFoundries 12nm FinFET implementation with a core area of 0.34 mm^2, 104 KB on-chip memory, and voltage scaling from 0.55 to 0.70 V achieves sub-millisecond latency, up to 117.0 BTOPS/W, and 0.07 pJ/SOP. On IBM DVS128 Gesture and N-MNIST, BitFair achieves 96.5% and 97.7% accuracy, respectively, while improving effective energy efficiency by 4.0-22.1x and accuracy by up to 9.2% over prior fabricated XR vision accelerators.

</details>


### [67] [Neuromorphic Silicon Neuron Controller for Adaptive Deep Brain Stimulation in Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05453)
*Md Abu Bakr Siddique,Jakub Orłowski,Yan Zhang,Hongyu An*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SiLIF-DBS控制器，一种基于CMOS的神经形态硅神经元刺激器，用于帕金森病的自适应深部脑刺激。该系统以STN-LFPs的Beta ARV作为控制生物标记，在闭环验证中实现了显著的病理beta活动抑制，功耗仅为开环刺激的25%，抑制效率达5.85%/μW，为低功耗植入式aDBS系统提供了可行基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病影响全球数百万人并导致严重的运动症状。虽然自适应深部脑刺激（aDBS）能够追踪PD运动症状的波动并实现更智能的DBS控制，但现有方法主要基于算法和软件，在电路实现方面尤其是低功耗植入式集成电路方面的研究有限，这促使了本研究开发硬件级的解决方案。

Method: 提出了硅泄漏整合发放深部脑刺激（SiLIF-DBS）控制器，这是一种采用金属氧化物半导体（CMOS）技术实现的神经形态硅神经元刺激器。推导了SiLIF-DBS控制器的简化计算模型，并将其嵌入帕金森皮质-基底节框架中进行闭环验证。系统以beta波段丘脑底核局部场电位（STN-LFPs）为驱动，以其平均整流值（Beta ARV）作为控制生物标记。

Result: SiLIF-DBS控制器成功抑制了病理性beta活动，功耗仅为开环刺激的25%，抑制效率达到5.85%/μW。在大幅降低功耗的同时实现了显著的beta抑制效果，展现出高抑制效率。

Conclusion: SiLIF-DBS控制器在大幅降低功耗的同时实现了显著的beta抑制效果，具有高抑制效率，证明了其作为低功耗植入式aDBS可行基础的价值，为帕金森病的自适应深部脑刺激提供了硬件级的解决方案。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) affects millions worldwide and causes severe motor symptoms. Adaptive deep brain stimulation (aDBS) delivers physiologically informed stimulation that can track fluctuations in PD motor symptoms, enabling more intelligent DBS control. However, most existing aDBS approaches are primarily algorithm- and software-driven, with limited efforts toward circuit realization, particularly low-power and implantable integrated circuits. This paper presents the Silicon Leaky Integrate-and-Fire Deep Brain Stimulation (SiLIF-DBS) controller, a neuromorphic silicon neuron stimulator implemented with metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) technology. For system-level evaluation, a simplified computational model of the SiLIF-DBS controller is derived and embedded within a Parkinsonian cortico-basal ganglia framework for closed-loop validation. The system is driven by beta-band subthalamic nucleus local field potentials (STN-LFPs), with their average rectified value (Beta ARV) used as the control biomarker. Our SiLIF-DBS controller for aDBS suppresses pathological beta activity while consuming only 25% of the power required by open-loop stimulation and achieving a suppression efficiency of $5.85\%$/$μ$W. Overall, our SiLIF-DBS controller achieves strong beta suppression at substantially reduced power, delivering high suppression efficiency that demonstrates it is a viable foundation for low-power implantable aDBS.

</details>


### [68] [Is Your NPU Ready for LLMs? Dissecting the Hidden Efficiency Bottlenecks in Mobile LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05475)
*Guanyu Cai,Ruiming Tian,Lang Yang,Zhouhong Ren,Jinliang Yuan,Lingkun Li,Jiliang Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过跨层次测量研究移动端LLM推理，发现框架选择、后端特性和调度配置均对性能和能耗有显著影响，并提出了节能的最佳实践配置，可降低高达54.8%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备上部署大型语言模型（LLM）可以增强隐私保护并减少延迟，但受限于硬件效率瓶颈。现有研究缺乏对移动端LLM推理的全面跨层次测量分析，特别是跨框架和硬件后端的分析。

Method: 开发了PowerBench细粒度分析工具，首次提供后端特定的能耗归因分析，对五个主流框架（如llama.cpp, GENIE）和三种硬件后端（CPU, GPU, NPU）进行了全面的跨层次测量研究。

Result: 发现了三个关键见解：（1）框架引起的性能差距在NPU上显著放大，使用自定义算子可达10倍；（2）存在明显的阶段划分：NPU擅长计算密集的预填充阶段，而CPU在内存密集的解码阶段表现更优；（3）后端特定分析发现调度存在优化空间，不当配置导致高达40%的能耗浪费。提出的最佳实践配置可在NPU后端降低高达54.8%的能耗。

Conclusion: 移动端LLM推理的性能和能耗优化需要跨框架、跨硬件后端的协同考虑。NPU在处理固定形状的大工作负载时表现优异，但不适合小内核、动态特性的解码阶段。通过优化调度配置，可以显著降低能耗。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on mobile devices enhances privacy and reduces latency, but is severely bottlenecked by hardware inefficiency. We present the first comprehensive, cross-layer measurement study of mobile LLM inference, uniquely spanning five mainstream frameworks (e.g., llama.cpp, GENIE) and three hardware backends (CPU, GPU, NPU). To enable this analysis, we develop PowerBench, a fine-grained profiling tool that provides the first backend-specific energy attribution, moving beyond traditional device-level measurements. Our study yields three critical insights: (1) Framework-induced performance gaps are substantially amplified on NPUs, reaching up to 10x using custom operators due to divergent offloading and quantization strategies. (2) We identify a distinct phase split where NPUs excel at compute-bound prefilling, while CPUs outperform all other backends in memory-bound decoding. This is driven by the NPU's preference for large, fixed-shape workloads, which conflicts with the small-kernel, dynamic nature of decoding. (3) Backend-specific profiling uncovers substantial scheduling headroom missed by prior work. Suboptimal thread configurations, uncoordinated NPU sleep latencies, and CPU polling intervals result in up to 40% energy waste. Leveraging these findings, we present an energy-oriented best-practice configuration for mobile LLM inference. We estimate that this configuration could reduce energy consumption by up to 54.8% on the NPU backend across three datasets.

</details>


### [69] [NEMESIS: NEtlist-Driven Modeling and Equation Synthesis with Inversion-Aware SPICE Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05657)
*Subhadip Ghosh,Ramesh Harjani,Sachin S. Sapatnekar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了NEMESIS框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）设计运算跨导放大器（OTA）。该方法在快速解析模型与精确但计算昂贵的SPICE仿真之间取得平衡，在65nm工艺下实现了平均相对误差小于7%、评估速度提升约4622倍的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的OTA设计面临两难选择：快速的解析模型精度不足，而精确的SPICE仿真计算开销过大。本研究旨在开发一种能够平衡设计精度与计算效率的自动化框架，加速模拟电路设计流程。

Method: NEMESIS采用多模态方法，输入OTA网表和原理图后识别电路原语并生成性能方程。框架优先从历史相似OTA中检索方程作为初始解，若无历史数据则由LLM直接推导初始方程。随后通过基于SPICE的修复循环对方程进行迭代细化，逐步提高方程精度。

Result: 在商用65nm PDK上，NEMESIS成功应用于五种OTA拓扑结构。生成的性能方程经SPICE验证，在偏置范围内平均相对误差小于7%，收敛后的评估速度相比完整的SPICE仿真提升约4622倍。

Conclusion: NEMESIS框架有效结合了LLM的推理能力与SPICE仿真验证，成功实现了高精度、高效率的OTA性能建模，为模拟电路自动化设计提供了新的解决方案，显著提升了设计效率。

Abstract: This work presents NEMESIS, a multimodal framework for operational transconductance amplifier (OTA) design using large language models (LLMs). NEMESIS strikes a balance between fast, approximate analytical models vs. accurate, computationally expensive SPICE evaluations. Given an OTA netlist and schematic, NEMESIS first identifies circuit primitives and then generates progressively more accurate performance equations. The framework begins with equations retrieved from the prior invocations of NEMESIS to structurally similar OTAs, if available; otherwise, it uses the LLM to derive the initial equations directly from the circuit input. These equations are iteratively refined via a SPICE-based repair loop. In a commercial 65nm PDK, NEMESIS is demonstrated on five OTA topologies, producing SPICE-verified equations across biasing ranges with <7% average relative error and a post-convergence evaluation speedup of ~4622x over full SPICE-based evaluation.

</details>


### [70] [Boosting FPGA Performance with Direct BRAM-DSP Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05756)
*Jiajun Hu,Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa,Andrew Boutros,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的FPGA架构增强方案，通过在BRAM和DSP块之间引入专用直接连接来优化深度学习工作负载的性能，在不影响商业可行性的前提下实现了最高25%的Fmax提升和49%的布线长度减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代FPGA设计中，特别是深度学习工作负载，BRAM与DSP之间的数据传输必须经过全局布线网络，导致布线长度增加、布线拥塞和关键路径延迟，成为性能瓶颈。现有解决方案需要根本性改变FPGA架构和CAD工具，商业可行性有限。

Method: 提出轻量级架构增强：1) 在BRAM和DSP块之间引入专用直接连接，使BRAM数据无需经过全局互连即可被DSP消费；2) 增强布局算法以识别BRAM-DSP宏块。该方案保持与基线架构的兼容性。

Result: 在类Agilex-10 FPGA上，常见深度学习层设计实现了最高+25%的Fmax提升和-49%的布线长度减少。架构改动带来可忽略的面积和延迟开销，不影响非DL基准测试，且提出的CAD方案在基线架构上QoR变化可忽略。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级架构增强方案成功解决了深度学习工作负载的数据传输瓶颈，在保持商业可行性的同时显著提升了性能，无需对FPGA架构和CAD工具进行根本性改变。

Abstract: Efficient data movement between memory and compute units is a key performance bottleneck in modern FPGA designs, particularly for deep learning (DL) workloads. In typical FPGA architectures, data transfers between block RAMs (BRAMs) and digital signal processing units (DSPs) must traverse the global routing network, leading to increased wirelength, routing congestion, and critical-path delays. Prior work has explored in- and near-BRAM compute architectures to mitigate these issues, but such solutions often require fundamental changes to FPGA architecture and CAD tools, limiting their commercial viability. This paper proposes a lightweight architectural enhancement that introduces a dedicated direct connection between BRAM and DSP blocks, enabling BRAM data to be consumed by DSPs without passing through the global interconnect. We also enhance the placement algorithm to recognize these BRAM-DSP macro blocks. The proposed architectural change incurs negligible area and delay overhead and does not affect non-DL benchmarks, while the proposed CAD remains compatible with the baseline architecture, where it yields negligible change in quality-of-results (QoR). On an Agilex-10-like FPGA, the proposed architecture and CAD updates deliver up to +25% Fmax and -49% wirelength on common DL layer designs.

</details>


### [71] [GPU-Accelerated Effective Resistance Analysis for 3D IC Power Delivery Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05818)
*Jingchao Hu,Cheng Zhuo,Zhou Jin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个GPU加速框架，用于早期3D集成电路PDN的有效电阻分析，相比传统直接求解器实现了5-6个数量级的加速，同时保持了可忽略的误差偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电流需求和设计复杂度的增加，3D集成电路中的功率传输网络（PDN）面临日益严峻的挑战。不合理的硅通孔（TSV）规划会导致有效电阻分布不均，进而引发严重的IR Drop问题，因此需要快速准确的有效电阻分析方法。

Method: 提出了一个基于GPU加速的框架，用于早期3D IC PDN的有效电阻分析，通过GPU并行计算实现对大规模PDN网络的快速求解。

Result: 相比传统直接求解器实现了5到6个数量级的加速，同时在最大和平均相对误差方面保持了可忽略的偏差。

Conclusion: 该GPU加速框架能够为早期3D IC设计提供快速且准确的有效电阻分析，有助于TSV的合理规划，从而提升PDN的可靠性和IR Drop性能。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) integration is a critical technique for enhancing transistor density, improving power efficiency, and reducing interconnect delays. However, as current demands and design complexity increase, power deliver networks (PDNs) are facing growing challenges.Careful planning of through-silicon vias (TSVs) is essential for ensuring reliable PDNs, where effective resistance serves as a vital metric for the reliability. Ill-planned TSVs often cause 3D IC with unevenly distributed effective resistance and consequently severer IR Drop.In this paper, we propose a GPU-accelerated framework on accurate effective resistance analysis for early stage 3D IC PDNs. The proposed framework achieves a speedup of 5 to 6 orders of magnitude compared to the conventional direct solver, while maintaining negligible deviations in both maximum and average relative errors.

</details>

### [72] [Complets: Universal Compartmentalisation and Programming Model For Arm Permission Overlay Extension 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05569)
*Vasily A. Sartakov*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文分析了ARM POE2进程内隔离机制，提出了一个通用编程模型和安全模型，抽象了空间和时间索引的复杂性，为POE2系统提供了安全可靠的编程方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POE2作为基于内存保护键的进程内隔离机制，虽然提供了比POE1更强的安全保证，但引入了相当大的架构复杂性。有效权限来自多个硬件组件的交互，使得直接编程变得非平凡且容易产生微妙错误。

Method: 对POE2机制进行详细分析，引入一个通用编程模型和强安全模型，抽象空间和时间索引的复杂性，同时支持使用进程内隔离构建的典型分区软件模式。

Result: 提出了适用于POE2系统的通用编程模型和安全模型，该模型能够抽象复杂的空间和时间索引，同时支持典型的分区软件模式。

Conclusion: 该编程模型有效降低了POE2系统的编程复杂性，提供了强安全保证，使得开发人员能够更安全、可靠地构建基于进程内隔离的分区软件系统。

Abstract: Arm Permission Overlay Extension (POE) is an intra-process isolation mechanism based on memory protection keys. This mechanism partitions virtual memory into regions whose access permissions can be reconfigured without invoking higher exception levels. Because POE does not enforce privilege separation, POE-based protection domains are applicable only as a software fault-isolation technique.
  The POE2 is an isolation mechanism that substantially extends POE1. Dealing with memory protection keys, it also introduces new registers and tables that regulate the operations permitted for code executing within protection domains associated with different keys. A permission is determined by the spatial index of the executing code (i.e., the protection key associated with code), the spatial index of the accessed memory, the operation type, and a new attribute of the thread context: its temporal index.
  POE2 provides stronger security guarantees than POE1, but also introduces considerable architectural complexity. Effective permissions arise from the interaction of multiple hardware components, making direct programming non-trivial and prone to subtle errors. In the paper, we present a detailed analysis of POE2 and introduce a universal programming model with a strong security model for POE2-based systems. The model abstracts the complexity of spatial and temporal indices while enabling typical patterns of partitioned software constructed using intra-process isolation.

</details>

### [73] [Statistically Meaningful Geometry and Gauge Symmetry Breaking: A Geometric Foundation for Scientific Discovery and Intelligence Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05436)
*Bing Cheng,Yi-Shuai Niu,Howell Tong,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统计有意义几何(SMG)框架，证明过参数化学习系统在面对未建模因果机制时会发生几何崩溃，触发规范对称性破缺，通过结构G熵的整数阶跃来区分真正的智能发现与统计模式匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化机器学习架构（特别是大语言模型）的快速扩展引发根本性危机：这些系统是否展现真正的智能，还是仅仅是复杂的统计模式匹配器？经典平坦欧几里得统计无法区分连续插值与自主发现新颖因果规律。

Method: 引入统计有意义几何(SMG)框架，将过参数化学习系统建模为无限维非参数Orlicz纤维丛。通过几何分析证明，在未建模因果机制驱动的持续分布外刺激下，连续优化失败，未建模方差被拒绝并泄漏到不可观察的垂直纤维空间，积累主动非因果张力，最终触发共轭焦点边界并导致矩阵奇异性。

Result: 证明几何崩溃作为严格的非平衡触发器引发规范对称性破缺(GSB)，系统从不可观察的规范冗余中清除隐藏张力，自发结晶出新的数学独立的水平坐标轴，表现为可观测量结构G熵的离散+1.0整数阶跃。通过参数图表解耦和最小能量路径准则、因果不变性过滤器，区分真正的发现与恶性幻觉。

Conclusion: SMG提供了一个无参数、可证伪的仪表板来数学认证真正的智能，将"AI for Science"转化为自主范式转变的引擎，为区分智能与统计模式匹配提供了严格的数学判据。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of over-parameterized machine learning architectures, particularly LLMs, raises a profound crisis: do these systems exhibit genuine intelligence, or are they merely sophisticated statistical pattern matchers? Classical flat Euclidean statistics cannot differentiate continuous interpolation from the autonomous discovery of novel causal laws. To resolve this, we introduce Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG), a framework modeling over-parameterized learning systems as infinite-dimensional non-parametric Orlicz fiber bundles. We prove that under persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) stimuli governed by unmodeled causal mechanisms, continuous optimization fails. Unmodeled variance is rejected by the visible horizontal base manifold, leaking into the unobservable vertical fiber space and generating an accumulation of Active Acausal Tension. Driven by the statistical manifold's non-linear curvature, this tension inevitably strikes a conjugate focal boundary ($T_{\text{crit}} = π^2 / K_{\text{max}}$), triggering localized volumetric collapse and a catastrophic matrix singularity ($[G_f]^{-1} \to \infty$). We demonstrate this geometric breakdown acts as the strict non-equilibrium trigger for a Gauge Symmetry Break (GSB). The system purges hidden tension from unobservable gauge redundancies, spontaneously crystallizing a new, mathematically independent horizontal coordinate axis. This non-parametric phase transition registers as a discrete $+1.0$ integer step-jump in observable Structural G-Entropy. By decoupling parameter charts and subjecting emergent axes to a Minimal Energy Path Criterion and a Causal Invariance Filter, we distinguish genuine discovery from malignant hallucinations. Ultimately, SMG provides a parameter-free, falsifiable dashboard to mathematically certify true intelligence, transforming AI for Science into an engine of autonomous paradigm shifts.

</details>


### [74] [PatchOptic for Shared-State LLM Workflows with Projected Views and Verified Structured Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05483)
*Zhaoyu Bai,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PatchOptic，一种受光学概念启发的接口，通过投影读取和验证结构化补丁，解决了共享状态LLM工作流中局部更新与全局有效性之间的契约问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM工作流通过关键词搜索、RAG、AST查询等方法实现了状态片段的读取，但缺乏局部更新应用到全局状态时的有效性验证机制，即缺少局部更新与全局有效性之间的契约。

Method: 提出PatchOptic接口，借用光学的组合双向访问器概念，通过投影读取和验证结构化补丁实现。每个工作流步骤声明投影读取视图、授权写入区域和补丁源区域，同一声明支持委托、子工作流组合和独立步骤重排序的静态证书。

Result: 在PatchBench基准（46个测试案例）上的评估表明：投影读取在保持输出质量的同时减少了信息泄露和token成本；运行时验证能在提交前阻止违反工作流契约的操作；补丁读取强制机制能拒绝使用隐藏源的受损补丁工件。

Conclusion: PatchOptic成功地将光学的视图/更新直觉应用于共享状态LLM工作流，为局部更新与全局有效性之间建立了契约，在保证输出质量的同时提高了安全性、降低了成本，并支持工作流的灵活组合与优化。

Abstract: Agentic workflows often operate over shared, structured state. Because LLM context windows are limited, each model invocation is typically shown only the state fragment needed for the current workflow step, a pattern commonly known as progressive disclosure. Modern systems construct such model-facing views using grep-like keyword search, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), abstract-syntax-tree (AST) queries, and task-specific agent skills. These methods make the read side manageable, but they do not define when a locally proposed rewrite is valid after it is applied back to the full state. The missing piece is a contract between local updates and global validity. We introduce PatchOptic, an optic-inspired interface for shared-state LLM workflows. Optics are compositional bidirectional accessors that describe how views of structured data are read and updated. PatchOptic borrows this view/update intuition and realizes it through projected reads and verified structured patches. Each workflow step declares a projected read view, an authorized write region, and a patch-source region. Beyond runtime enforcement, the same declaration yields a path-level footprint that supports delegation, sub-workflow composition, and static certificates for reordering independent steps within the same phase. We evaluate this design with PatchBench, a benchmark with 46 cases across domains. The results show that projected reads reduce reported leakage and token cost while preserving accepted-output quality under the strong actor. Runtime verification blocks declared workflow-contract violations before commit, and patch-read enforcement rejects compromised patch artifacts that use hidden sources.

</details>


### [75] [Design-CP: Context Parallelism for Design of Protein Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05439)
*Lorenzo Tarricone,Helen E. Eisenach,Aiko Muraishi,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Design-CP，一种针对RFdiffusion的上下文并行推理策略，通过分布式计算解决多聚体蛋白质复合物设计中的GPU显存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全原子生成蛋白质模型在设计大型多聚体复合物时，由于链数和残基数增加，其二次方的token和原子对表示会迅速超出单GPU显存限制。

Method: 引入两种上下文并行(CP)推理策略：1D行分片和带环形注意力的2D网格分片，将二次方激活分布在多GPU网格上，同时保持预训练权重不变。

Result: 在二十面体组装体采样中，最大可行的非对称单元(ASU)大小随GPU数量呈预期的平方根趋势增长；2D分片实现更好的时钟时间扩展；强点群对称约束使CP能直接用于二十面体纳米颗粒的端到端全原子设计；在工作站级16GB GPU小集群上展示了八面体纳米颗粒设计。

Conclusion: Design-CP为普及大型蛋白质组装体设计提供了实用路径，使研究者在有限的计算资源下也能进行复杂多聚体设计。

Abstract: Many all-atom generative protein models can in principle design large multimeric complexes by jointly modelling all chains, but their quadratic token- and atom-pair representations quickly exceed single-GPU memory as the number of chains and residues modelled grows. We introduce Design-CP, two context-parallel (CP) inference strategies for RFdiffusion 3 (1D row-sharding and 2D grid sharding with ring attention) that distribute the quadratic activations across a multi-GPU mesh while preserving pretrained weights. We characterise their scaling when sampling icosahedral assemblies, showing that the maximum feasible asymmetric subunit (ASU) size grows with the expected square-root trend in GPU count and that 2D sharding achieves better wall-clock scaling. Moreover, we show how strong point-group symmetry constraints make CP usable out of the box for end-to-end, all-atom design of icosahedral nanoparticles, yielding favourable in silico structural and interface metrics. Finally, we demonstrate octahedral nanoparticle design on a small cluster of workstation-grade 16GB GPUs, illustrating how Design-CP can be a practical path towards democratising large-assembly protein design.

</details>


### [76] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Battery Management of Autonomous Order Pickers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05683)
*Taniya Shaji,Abhay Sobhanan,Christof Defryn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习框架，用于解决仓库中自主移动机器人（AMR）在随机订单到达情况下的动态充电问题，使订单完成率相比最强基线提升高达6%，并显著减少了充电操作的总时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库中自主移动机器人（AMR）的充电是一个关键运营挑战，严重影响订单处理时间和吞吐量。传统的固定规则启发式算法在动态环境中通常表现次优，且未能考虑多AMR协调，导致严重的资源效率低下。因此，需要一种能够动态学习最优充电决策的方法。

Method: 提出了一个基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习（DRL）框架，专门针对具有固定充电站的多区块仓库。该模型动态学习两个关键决策：充电站选择和最优充电时长，并明确考虑了充电站的预期排队时间。

Result: 通过大量数值实验，将所提模型与最先进的DRL方法和传统启发式方法进行基准比较。结果表明，PPO框架相比最强基线将订单完成率提高了高达6%，同时显著减少了用于充电操作的总时间。模型在不同的仓库配置和随机到达率下也表现出稳健性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种有效的动态充电决策框架，解决了传统方法的局限性。通过对学习到的DRL策略进行解释，提供了有价值的运营见解，证明了其在标准基准之上的优越性，为仓库AMR充电管理提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Battery charging of Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) in warehouses is a critical operational challenge that heavily impacts both order processing times and throughput. In this study, we address the dynamic AMR charging problem under stochastic order arrivals, where robots must learn optimal charging decisions. Traditional fixed-rule heuristics often prove suboptimal in dynamic environments and fail to account for multi-AMR coordination, leading to severe resource inefficiencies. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) framework designed for multi-block warehouses with fixed charging stations. Our model dynamically learns two key decisions: charging station selection and optimal charging duration, explicitly accounting for anticipated queuing times at the stations. Extensive numerical experiments benchmark the proposed model against state-of-the-art DRL and traditional heuristic approaches. Results demonstrate that our PPO framework increases order-completion rates by up to 6\% compared to the strongest baseline, while significantly reducing the total time dedicated to recharging operations. Furthermore, we validate the model's robustness across diverse warehouse configurations and stochastic arrival rates. Finally, we interpret the learned DRL policy, offering valuable operational insights into its superiority over standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [77] [Geometry-Aware Infrastructure-Anchored Denoiser for UWB Sensing and Work-Zone Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05449)
*Weizhe Tang,Jiaxi Liu,Junwei you,Steven T. Parker,Pei Li,Sikai Chen,Meng Ran,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GAIA框架，一种几何感知的基础设施锚定学习方法，用于解决室外UWB测距中的非视距传播、突发噪声和长尾误差问题，在真实数据集上相比PoseMLP降低了18.4%的MSE，提升了15.5%的多边形IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的作业区几何感知对智能交通系统至关重要，超宽带（UWB）传感为基础设施辅助重建提供了一种低成本方法。然而，室外UWB测距常受到非视距传播、突发噪声和长尾误差的影响，这些问题会扭曲下游的空间重建质量，亟需一种有效的方法来提升测距精度和几何重建的一致性。

Method: 提出GAIA（几何感知、基础设施锚定学习框架），该框架将时间范围建模与潜在锚点布局估计和确定性距离投影相结合。GAIA将范围去噪作为监督任务，同时将学习到的距离引导向边界一致的重建方向，实现了测距精度与几何重建质量的联合优化。

Result: 在真实室外UWB数据集（包含同步的UWB、GNSS和IMU测量）以及真实数据校准的压力测试模拟器上进行评估。GAIA在所有基于滤波和基于学习的基线方法中取得了最低的整体范围MSE和最高的多边形IoU，相比PoseMLP方法MSE降低18.4%，多边形IoU提升15.5%。

Conclusion: 几何感知的范围去噪为空间一致的作业区重建提供了一条有效途径，证明了将几何约束融入测距去噪过程能够显著提升空间重建的质量和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate work-zone geometry perception is critical for intelligent transportation systems, and ultra-wideband sensing offers a low-cost approach for infrastructure-aided reconstruction. However, outdoor UWB ranging is often degraded by non-line-of-sight propagation, burst noise, and long-tail errors, which can distort downstream spatial reconstruction. We present GAIA, a geometry-aware, infrastructure-anchored learning framework that couples temporal range modeling with latent anchor-layout estimation and deterministic distance projection. GAIA preserves range denoising as the supervised task while orienting the learned distances toward boundary-consistent reconstruction. We evaluate GAIA on a real-world outdoor UWB dataset with synchronized UWB, GNSS, and IMU measurements, and further test robustness using a real-data-calibrated stress-test simulator. GAIA achieves the lowest overall range MSE and highest polygon IoU among evaluated filtering-based and learning-based baselines, reducing MSE by 18.4% and improving polygon IoU by 15.5% over PoseMLP. These results show that geometry-aware range denoising provides an effective path toward spatially coherent work-zone reconstruction.

</details>


### [78] [The Granularity Paradox: How Temporal Disaggregation Inflates In-Sample Fit and Compounds Out-of-Sample Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05450)
*Hugo Moreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了时间序列预测中的"粒度悖论"：细粒度数据虽增加样本量但导致递归误差累积，粗粒度避免误差传播但减少可用数据。通过10个模型和6种粒度的实证研究，发现LSTM在高频率下能克服误差传播，线性回归保持稳定，而传统递归模型性能急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中存在一个未被充分研究的权衡问题：数据的时间粒度选择会影响预测性能。细粒度分解（如月度到周/日度）虽然能增加样本量并改善样本内诊断，但由于递归误差在更长预测期上的累积，会导致样本外精度下降；相反，粗粒度聚合（年度）虽能消除递归误差传播，但会减少估计器可用的数据量。研究旨在形式化这一权衡并系统性评估不同模型在不同粒度下的表现差异。

Method: 使用13年的公共采购数据集，跨越6种时间粒度（从日度到年度），对10个模型进行基准测试。这些模型包括朴素模型、统计模型、机器学习和深度学习架构。研究引入了"共识-异议"诊断方法，比较点指标（RMSE、MAE）与累积指标（TPFE）在不同粒度下的方向性行为，以识别标准诊断掩盖系统性误差传播的模型。

Result: 实证结果揭示了非单调的阈值结构：递归自回归和季节性模型在高频率预测下性能显著下降（如Holt-Winters在日度粒度下测试R²为-151，TPFE达425.85%）；LSTM呈现U型误差曲线，从月度（19.66%）到双周（35.94%）性能恶化，但在日度粒度下克服误差传播惩罚（TPFE为4.35%，R²为0.66）；线性回归在所有粒度下保持稳定（TPFE为16.3-17.0%）。这证实了悖论由递归反馈拓扑驱动，而非模型复杂性。

Conclusion: 研究证明标准点指标（RMSE、MAE）系统性掩盖累积误差传播，仅使用这类指标评估预测会导致对模型充分性的误判。粒度悖论主要由递归反馈拓扑结构驱动，不同模型在不同粒度下表现差异显著。引入的共识-异议诊断方法能够有效识别标准诊断掩盖系统性误差传播的模型，为时间序列预测模型选择和评估提供了新的理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: This paper explores the "Granularity Paradox" in time-series forecasting, wherein finer temporal disaggregation (e.g., Monthly to Weekly/Daily) improves in-sample diagnostics and dataset size (N), but degrades out-of-sample accuracy due to recursive error compounding over longer horizons (H). Conversely, coarse aggregation (Annual) eliminates recursive error propagation but reduces data available to estimators. We formalize this trade-off and benchmark 10 models - spanning naïve, statistical, machine learning, and deep learning architectures - across six granularities using a 13-year public procurement dataset. The empirical results reveal a non-monotonic threshold structure: recursive autoregressive and seasonal models degrade substantially under high-frequency forecasting (e.g., Holt-Winters reaches a Test R-squared of -151 and TPFE of 425.85% at the Daily grain), while the LSTM traces a U-shaped error curve, worsening from Monthly (19.66%) through Bi-Weekly (35.94%) before overcoming the error propagation penalty at Daily (TPFE of 4.35%, R-squared of 0.66). Linear Regression remains stable across all granularities (16.3-17.0% TPFE), confirming that the paradox is driven by recursive feedback topology, not model complexity. The results demonstrate that standard pointwise metrics (RMSE, MAE) systematically mask cumulative error propagation, and that evaluating forecasts without goal-dependent cumulative metrics produces misleading assessments of model adequacy. We introduce a consensus-dissensus diagnostic comparing the directional behaviour of pointwise metrics against cumulative TPFE across granularities, enabling the identification of models whose standard diagnostics mask systematic error propagation.

</details>


### [79] [Exogenous Dropout: A Simple, Strong Baseline for Corruption-Robust Time Series Forecasting with Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05452)
*Hao Hu,Xue-shan Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种简单且模型无关的"外生变量丢弃"训练方法，通过在训练时随机置零外生变量通道，显著提升了时间序列预测模型在外生变量受损时的鲁棒性，且效果优于专门设计的架构方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用外生协变量的时间序列预测模型在部署时表现脆弱：当外生变量受到噪声干扰、时间错位或缺失时，强依赖外生变量的模型性能会严重退化，甚至不如仅使用内生变量的基准模型。研究旨在探索是否需要专门的架构来获得鲁棒性，还是可以通过简单的训练干预实现。

Method: 提出"外生变量丢弃"（exogenous dropout）方法：一种模型无关的训练干预策略，在训练过程中随机将整个外生变量通道置零。该方法不需要对模型架构进行特殊设计，可应用于任何使用外生变量的时间序列预测模型。研究将其与专门设计的有界架构Baseline——BoundEx（结合可学习门控、回退残差连接和逐通道FiLM调制）进行对比。

Result: 在电价预测、水库水文学和气象学三个领域的实验中，外生变量丢弃方法在高斯噪声、时间错位和通道完全缺失情况下显著提升了模型鲁棒性，同时保持了在干净数据上的准确率。应用于双相关网络时产生了最鲁棒的模型，在所有领域都优于有界架构BoundEx。消融实验、门控行为诊断和表示层面的分析表明，显式的架构有界性并非获得鲁棒性的必要条件。

Conclusion: 显式的架构有界性对于获得外生变量鲁棒性并非必要：使用外生变量丢弃训练的无界模型在所有领域都比有界模型更鲁棒。外生变量丢弃是一个简单、强力的基准方法，推荐作为未来使用协变量的时间序列预测研究的标准做法。研究同时发布了损坏鲁棒性基准测试。

Abstract: Time series forecasters that use exogenous covariates are fragile in deployment: when those covariates are noised, temporally misaligned, or missing, strong exogenous-fusion and exogenous-adapted models can degrade far above the endogenous-only floor. We study whether such robustness requires specialized architectures, or whether it can be obtained through a simple training intervention. We propose exogenous dropout, a model-agnostic method that randomly zeros whole exogenous channels during training. Across electricity-price forecasting, reservoir hydrology, and meteorology, exogenous dropout substantially improves robustness under Gaussian noise, temporal misalignment, and fully missing channels, while preserving clean accuracy. Applied to a dual-correlation network, it yields the most robust model in our experiments, outperforming a deliberately strong bounded architectural foil, BoundEx, which combines a learnable gate, a fallback residual to the endogenous backbone, and per-channel exogenous FiLM modulation. Architecture-by-dropout ablations, gate-behavior diagnostics, and a representation-level bound show that explicit architectural boundedness is not necessary for this robustness: an unbounded model trained with exogenous dropout is more robust than the bounded model in every domain. We release a corruption-robustness benchmark and recommend exogenous dropout as a simple, strong baseline for future work on time series forecasting with covariates.

</details>


### [80] [Empirical Minimal-Realisation Compression of Deep Neural Networks via Controllability-Observability Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05457)
*Anis Hamadouche,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可控性-可观测性框架的深度神经网络压缩方法，通过数据驱动的Gramian矩阵估计层级可达/可观秩，实现模型状态维度的大幅压缩（70%以上），同时几乎不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络存在大量隐藏状态冗余，但现有压缩方法（剪枝、量化等）直接操作权重或神经元，未能显式刻画内部状态的动态作用，缺乏从动力系统角度的原理性压缩指导。

Method: 将训练好的网络视为按深度索引的非线性动力系统，利用隐藏状态快照和输出Jacobian构建数据驱动的可达性、可观测性和平衡Gramian矩阵，计算各层的可达秩、可观秩及联合可达-可观秩，以此作为层宽压缩的准则，实现平衡实现形式的状态阶次缩减。

Result: 在MNIST上，四层SiLU DNN状态维度从1024降至277（压缩72.95%），参数压缩73.48%，精度从96.60%保持至95.45%；在CIFAR-10上，状态维度从4608降至1339（压缩70.94%），参数压缩83.09%，精度保持在54.44%，CUDA推理延迟降低约3倍。方法优于投影降维、结构化/非结构化剪枝、低秩SVD、INT8量化等基线。

Conclusion: 平衡的可达-可观秩提供了有原则的经验最小实现准则，可用于设计紧凑的神经架构，在大幅压缩状态维度和参数量的同时几乎不损失精度，为神经网络压缩提供了新的控制理论视角。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often contain substantial hidden-state redundancy, but most compression methods operate directly on weights, neurons, or quantised representations without explicitly characterising the dynamical role of internal states. This paper proposes a controllability-observability framework for empirical state-order reduction of deep neural networks. By viewing a trained network as a depth-indexed nonlinear dynamical system, we construct data-driven reachability, observability, and balanced Gramians from hidden-state snapshots and output Jacobians. The resulting A/B/C tests estimate layer-wise reachable, observable, and jointly reachable--observable ranks. These ranks are then used not only as diagnostic measures of hidden-state redundancy, but also as actual compressed layer widths for realised reduced networks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 compare the proposed balanced realisation against projection-based reduction, unstructured pruning, structured pruning, low-rank SVD, dynamic INT8 quantisation, and linear baselines. On MNIST, a four-layer SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 1024 to 277, giving 72.95% state compression and 73.48% parameter compression, while maintaining 95.45% accuracy compared with 96.60% for the full model. On CIFAR-10, a larger SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 4608 to 1339, giving 70.94% state compression and 83.09% parameter compression, while preserving accuracy from 54.45% to 54.44% and reducing CUDA inference latency by approximately 3X. The results show that balanced reachable-observable ranks provide a principled empirical minimal-realisation criterion for designing compact neural architectures with little or no loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [Learning to Control LLM Agent Harnesses with Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05458)
*Haiwen Yi,Xinyuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM代理的执行框架（harness）视为可学习的控制层，通过轻量级控制器选择结构化执行动作，在冻结LLM执行器的情况下实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理改进方法主要关注提示词、模型或手工工作流的优化，而将执行框架视为固定基础设施。作者认为执行框架本身是一个可学习的控制层，具有改进潜力。

Method: 将框架操作形式化为有限时域Harness MDP，训练轻量级控制器从离线rollouts中学习结构化执行动作。使用advantage-weighted regression方法，仅依赖终端任务rubric奖励。引入Harness Maturity Score衡量框架是否遵循可靠执行模式，与最终任务质量分离。

Result: 在6个控制域和2个公共基准适配器上，学习的控制器持续改进验证行为并有选择性地提升最终任务质量。在adapted tau-bench retail、adapted AgentBench DB-Bench和带有校准结构化验证器的编程任务上获得最大提升。消融实验证明收益不源于模仿学习或简单添加检查。

Conclusion: 框架控制是冻结LLM代理的可学习层，离线支持限制了更好的过程控制何时转化为更好的最终答案。为LLM代理优化提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are usually improved by changing prompts, models, or hand-written workflows, while the execution harness around the model is treated as fixed infrastructure. We argue that this harness is itself a learnable control layer. We formalize harness operation as a finite-horizon Harness MDP, where a lightweight controller selects structural execution actions while the LLM executor remains frozen. The controller is trained from offline rollouts using advantage-weighted regression with only terminal task-rubric rewards. We also separate final task quality from a post-hoc Harness Maturity Score, which measures whether the harness follows reliable execution patterns rather than only whether the final answer is correct. This separation gives a finite-buffer view of harness learning: final-quality gains require high-return support in the offline buffer, while process behavior can shift whenever it aligns with advantage-weighted actions. Across six controlled domains and two public-benchmark adapters, the learned controller consistently improves verification behavior and selectively improves final task quality, with the largest gains on adapted tau-bench retail, adapted AgentBench DB-Bench, and coding with a calibrated structural verifier. Ablations against behavior cloning and Forced CHECK show that the gains are not explained by imitation or by simply adding checks. These results identify harness control as a learnable layer for frozen LLM agents, while showing that offline support limits when better process control becomes better final answers.

</details>


### [82] [AdaStop: Cost-Aware Early Stopping for DNN Test Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05461)
*Bonan Shen,Wei-Jung Huang,Xin Liu,Jiazhou Gao,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AdaStop框架，解决了深度神经网络测试中的停止问题，通过成本-收益决策过程在发现率下降时自动停止测试，仅用9-31%的标注预算即可发现65-84%的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度神经网络测试方法主要关注在固定标注预算下发现模型故障，但在实际应用中选择合适的预算非常困难：测试过少会遗漏故障，测试过多则会带来不必要的标注成本。因此需要研究DNN测试中的停止问题。

Method: 将测试建模为成本-收益决策过程，其中标注输入产生成本c，发现故障产生价值v。提出AdaStop框架，在测试过程中估计边际故障发现率，当估计的发现率低于阈值τ=c/v时停止标注。

Result: 在多个数据集、架构和选择策略上的实验表明，使用仅9-31%的标注预算即可发现65-84%的故障，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: AdaStop框架成功解决了DNN测试中的停止问题，通过成本-收益分析实现了测试效率和效果的良好平衡，为实际应用中的测试预算决策提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: Existing methods for testing deep neural networks (DNNs) primarily prioritize test inputs likely to reveal model faults under a fixed labeling budget. In practice, choosing that budget is difficult: too little testing misses failures, while too much incurs unnecessary labeling costs. This work studies the stopping problem in DNN testing. We formulate testing as a cost--benefit decision process in which labeling an input incurs cost $c$ and discovering a fault yields value $v$. Based on this formulation, we introduce \textit{AdaStop}, a framework that estimates the marginal fault discovery rate during testing and stops labeling when the estimated rate falls below the threshold $τ= c/v$. Experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and selection strategies show that $65$--$84\%$ of faults can be discovered using only $9$--$31\%$ of the labeling budget.

</details>


### [83] [Learnable Weighting of Intra-Attribute Distances for Categorical Data Clustering with Nominal and Ordinal Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05464)
*Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分类数据聚类中序数属性和名义属性处理不当的问题，提出了一种新颖的距离度量和聚类算法，通过统一方式度量属性内距离并保留序数值的顺序关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分类数据聚类方法在计算差异性时通常将名义属性和序数属性同等对待，忽略了序数值的相对顺序信息；此外，名义和序数属性之间存在相互依赖关系，值得探索用于指示差异性。

Method: 从类似图的视角研究名义和序数属性值的内在差异和联系，提出一种新的距离度量方法，以统一方式度量名义和序数属性的属性内距离，同时保留序数值之间的顺序关系；进而提出一种新的聚类算法，将属性内距离权重的学习和数据对象的划分整合到单一学习范式而非两个独立步骤中。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的算法与现有同类方法相比具有有效性。

Conclusion: 通过统一处理名义和序数属性并保留序数顺序信息，所提出的距离度量和聚类算法能够有效提升分类数据聚类的性能，避免了分步学习导致的次优解问题。

Abstract: The success of categorical data clustering generally much relies on the distance metric that measures the dissimilarity degree between two objects. However, most of the existing clustering methods treat the two categorical subtypes, i.e. nominal and ordinal attributes, in the same way when calculating the dissimilarity without considering the relative order information of the ordinal values. Moreover, there would exist interdependence among the nominal and ordinal attributes, which is worth exploring for indicating the dissimilarity. This paper will therefore study the intrinsic difference and connection of nominal and ordinal attribute values from a perspective akin to the graph. Accordingly, we propose a novel distance metric to measure the intra-attribute distances of nominal and ordinal attributes in a unified way, meanwhile preserving the order relationship among ordinal values. Subsequently, we propose a new clustering algorithm to make the learning of intra-attribute distance weights and partitions of data objects into a single learning paradigm rather than two separate steps, whereby circumventing a suboptimal solution. Experiments show the efficacy of the proposed algorithm in comparison with the existing counterparts.

</details>


### [84] [Breaking Structural Isolation: Scalable Graph Clustering via Community-Aware Sampling and Structural Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05469)
*Jingyun Zhang,Hao Peng,Jianxin Li,Angsheng Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCISE框架，通过结合社区感知采样与受限结构熵来解决图对比学习中的“结构隔离”问题，显著提升了无监督图聚类的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督图聚类方法在使用图对比学习时，面临“结构隔离”问题：在mini-batch训练过程中难以捕捉表征全局拓扑分布的凝聚社区结构，导致社区碎片化且全局信息丢失。

Method: 提出SCISE框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 结构熵社区约束算子(SECC)，通过优化受限解空间内的结构信息来缓解社区碎片化；2) 社区感知采样扩展机制(CSampE)，将目标节点的社区上下文纳入采样批次，保留拓扑完整性；3) 结构对比学习模块(StructCL)，基于批次内结构相似性优化边权重，引导编码器在高阶结构空间学习表示。

Result: 在六个主流基准数据集上的实验表明，SCISE显著优于最先进算法；消融研究和鲁棒性分析进一步验证了其在大规模真实图上的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: SCISE成功解决了图对比学习中结构隔离的关键挑战，为大规模网络的无监督聚类提供了有效的解决方案，在保持结构完整性方面展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised graph clustering is a fundamental technique for uncovering underlying semantic patterns in large-scale networks. Although Graph Contrastive Learning has demonstrated promising performance, existing methods often suffer from the "structural isolation" issue during mini-batch training, making it challenging to capture cohesive community structures that characterize the global topological distribution. To address these challenges, we propose SCISE, a Scalable unsupervised graph Clustering framework that preserves structural Integrity by synergizing community-aware sampling with constrained Structural Entropy. Specifically, we first introduce the Structural Entropy Community Constraint operator (SECC), which optimizes structural information within a constrained solution space to mitigate community fragmentation and enhance partition cohesion. Second, to prevent global information loss during batch training, we design a Community-Aware Sampling Expansion (CSampE) mechanism that incorporates the community context of target nodes into sampling batches, effectively breaking structural barriers and preserving topological integrity. Finally, we devise a Structural Contrastive Learning (StructCL) module that refines edge weights based on intra-batch structural similarity, guiding the encoder to learn representations in a higher-order structural space. Extensive experiments on six mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCISE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms, with ablation studies and robustness analyses further validating its effectiveness and reliability for real-world large-scale graphs.

</details>


### [85] [Parameter-Free Encoders Remain Viable for RDB Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05476)
*Linjie Xu,David Wipf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验验证，证明了在关系数据库预测任务中，参数自由的简单编码器就能达到与复杂预训练编码器相当的性能，无需针对RDB进行特定的预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关系数据库中进行缺失值或未来值预测时，需要解决基础模型编码器的最优设计问题。现有研究存在分歧：一方认为参数自由的子图编码器结合单表基础模型即可达到接近最优性能，另一方则主张使用参数化编码器并通过预训练利用可观测标签学习任务特定表示。为了消除这种模糊性，需要明确RDB编码器的设计原则。

Method: 通过理论分析方法，研究当标签作为输入存在时RDB编码器的性质，证明可训练编码器参数在效能上的局限性。同时，通过实验验证方法，在多个相关基准任务上测试简单的参数自由编码器的性能表现。

Result: 理论分析证明可训练编码器参数的有效性存在局限性；实验结果表明，相当简单的参数自由编码器在众多相关基准任务中仍然能够实现强大的性能表现，与复杂的参数化预训练编码器相当。

Conclusion: 在关系数据库预测任务中，参数自由的简单编码器设计是可行且高效的选择，能够避免RDB特定预训练的复杂性，同时保持与参数化编码器相当的性能水平，为实际应用提供了更简洁的解决方案。

Abstract: Given a relational database (RDB) storing heterogeneous tabular information, how can we predict missing (or future) values in some target column of interest? As the space of potential targets is vast across enterprise settings, it is preferable to avoid learning a new model from scratch each time there is a new prediction task. Frozen foundation models based on RDB-specific encoders provide a viable solution, but ideal design remains an open question. On the one hand, it has recently been argued that certain parameter-free subgraph encoders combined with single-table foundation models can achieve near SOTA performance, with no RDB-specific pre-training required. Meanwhile, other contemporary studies advocate for parameterized encoders pre-trained to exploit observable labels for learning task-specific representations. To address this ambiguity, we analyze RDB encoder properties specifically when labels are present as inputs, proving limitations on the potential efficacy of trainable encoder parameters. As empirical validation, we demonstrate that considerably simpler parameter-free encoders are still capable of strong performance across many relevant benchmarking tasks.

</details>


### [86] [InvWeaver: Deductive Feedback for Invariant Synthesis in Interacting-Loop Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05478)
*Guangyuan Wu,Weining Cao,Zehui Tan,Yuan Yao,Hengfeng Wei,Taolue Chen,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了InvWeaver，一个针对多循环程序的神经符号循环不变式合成框架，通过暴露循环间依赖并传播证明义务，在多循环基准测试中解决了72/82个问题，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM辅助猜测-检查技术在单循环程序上表现良好，但在处理包含多个交互循环的程序时往往表现不佳，这促使研究者开发能够处理多循环程序不变式推断的新方法。

Method: 提出InvWeaver神经符号框架，采用循环级抽象、义务引导推断和基于最弱前置条件的细化相结合的方法，暴露循环间依赖关系并传播证明义务。

Result: 在包含新构建的经典算法数据集的综合基准测试中，InvWeaver在82个多循环基准问题中解决了72个，显著优于现有不变式推断方法，同时在单循环任务上保持强劲性能。

Conclusion: InvWeaver成功解决了多循环程序不变式推断这一挑战性问题，为程序验证领域提供了一个有效的新工具，证明了神经符号方法在处理复杂程序结构方面的优势。

Abstract: Loop invariant inference is a fundamental yet challenging problem in program verification. Recent LLM-aided guess-and-check techniques have shown strong performance on single-loop programs, but they often struggle with programs containing multiple interacting loops. This paper presents InvWeaver, a neuro-symbolic framework for synthesizing invariants for such programs. The key idea is to expose inter-loop dependencies and propagate proof obligations through a combination of loop-level abstraction, obligation-guided inference, and weakest-precondition-based refinement. We evaluate InvWeaver on a comprehensive benchmark suite, including a newly curated dataset derived from classic algorithms. Experimental results show that InvWeaver substantially outperforms existing invariant inference methods, solving 72 out of 82 multi-loop benchmark problems and maintaining strong performance on single-loop tasks.

</details>


### [87] [$\mathbfλ$-VAE: Variance Equalization for Posterior Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05531)
*Girum Demisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了VAE后验崩溃的两个独立但耦合的成因（梯度失衡与信息间隙），并提出λ-VAE通过改进重参数化步骤实现方差均衡，有效缓解后验崩溃并显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器（VAE）经常遭受后验崩溃问题，即近似后验收敛到先验分布，导致潜在编码失去信息性。尽管已有大量研究，但关于崩溃发生的统一解释仍然缺失。作者旨在识别并形式化后验崩溃的根本原因，从而提出有效的解决方案。

Method: 作者识别并形式化了两个逻辑独立但耦合的成因：(1)梯度失衡，即解码器重建信号比KL正则化压力消失得更快；(2)信息间隙，即随机采样步骤丢弃大量编码器表示。基于此，提出λ-VAE方法，通过修改重参数化步骤：采样噪声按维度指数缩放，而KL惩罚保留原始后验方差。这种不对称性将稳定训练吸引子远离退化崩溃状态，驱动所有潜在维度达到相同均衡（方差均衡机制）。每个维度的最优指数由净信息增益目标推导，仅需一个超参数控制重建-生成权衡。

Result: 在Binary MNIST、Binary Omniglot、CIFAR-10、CelebA-64等标准基准上验证表明：该方法一致减少了崩溃维度数，信息容量增益高达2.8倍nats，重建质量提升高达+0.33 BPD。

Conclusion: 本研究首次统一解释了VAE后验崩溃的两个根本原因，并通过λ-VAE方法实现了理论驱动的解决方案。方差均衡机制成功将训练吸引子从退化状态转移，证明了该方法在多个基准数据集上的有效性，为理解和解决后验崩溃问题提供了新的理论框架和实践工具。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) frequently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which the approximate posterior converges to the prior, rendering the latent code uninformative. Despite extensive research, a unified account of why collapse occurs has remained an open question. We identify and formalize two logically independent but coupled causes. \emph{Gradient imbalance} occurs when the decoder's reconstruction signal vanishes faster than the $\mathbb{KL}$ regularization pressure as the posterior widens. \emph{Information gap} occurs when the stochastic sampling step discards a substantial fraction of the encoder's computed representation, attenuating decoder sensitivity and making collapse inexpensive. Both causes share the same collapse trajectory, and we show that the information gap is algebraically equivalent to mismatch between the aggregate posterior and the prior, unifying two pathologies. Subsequently, we introduce $λ$-VAE, which resolves both causes through a single modification to the reparameterization step: the sampling noise is scaled by per-dimension exponent, while the $\mathbb{KL}$ penalty retains the original posterior variance. This asymmetry shifts the stable training attractor away from the degenerate collapsed state, driving all latent dimensions toward the same equilibrium -- a mechanism we term \emph{variance equalization}. A closed-form optimal exponent per dimension follows from a net information gain objective, with a single hyperparameter controlling the reconstruction-generation tradeoff. We validate on standard benchmarks (Binary MNIST, Binary Omniglot, CIFAR-10, CelebA-64), showing consistent reductions in collapsed dimensions, information capacity gains of up to $2.8\times$ nats, and reconstruction quality improvements of up to $+0.33$ BPD.

</details>


### [88] [Self-Review Reinforcement Learning (SRRL) with Cross-Episode Memory and Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05541)
*Muhammad Zain Amin,Kibele Sebnem Yildirim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“自我审查强化学习”（SRRL）的训练框架，通过在强化学习过程中嵌入显式的自我审查步骤，解决了稀疏或延迟反馈下大语言模型难以定位错误和有效学习的问题。实验证明SRRL在GSM8K数据集上优于标准RLVR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在应用场景中，环境反馈通常是稀疏或延迟的，使得大语言模型难以确定推理过程中的哪些具体行动导致成功或失败。这种不确定性使得模型难以从失败中学习并进行有意义的行为修正，从而阻碍了强化学习在训练大语言模型中的有效性。

Method: 提出Self-Review Reinforcement Learning (SRRL)框架，在强化学习的每个训练轮次中嵌入显式的自我审查步骤。当模型的首次尝试失败时，模型会生成自我审查来识别错误，并据此条件化生成改进的第二次尝试。与推理时反思方法不同，SRRL使用策略梯度优化自我审查过程，并通过选择性蒸馏将改进内化到基础策略中，确保改进在未来的训练轮次中持续有效。框架还引入跨轮次记忆机制，保存成功的自我审查供未来类似任务复用。

Result: 在GSM8K基准测试上，使用GRPO优化器对Qwen 3-4B和OLMo-3-7B两个语言模型进行评估。SRRL在最终奖励性能上始终优于标准RLVR基线，并通过成功将反馈转化为行为改进实现了更高的学习效率。

Conclusion: SRRL框架通过显式自我审查和跨轮次记忆机制，有效地解决了稀疏反馈下大语言模型的强化学习难题，实现了反馈到行为改进的有效转化，为大语言模型在复杂任务中的强化学习训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning is commonly used to train large language models using environmental feedback. In applied settings, the environment usually provides sparse or delayed feedback. This makes it difficult for the model to pinpoint which actions in its reasoning led to success or failure. So, learning effectively from these signals is hard because the model must determine how each failure should inform meaningful behavioral corrections in subsequent iterations. We introduce a training framework, Self-Review Reinforcement Learning, that embeds an explicit self-review step into each RL episode. When a first-pass response fails, the model generates a self-review to identify what went wrong, which conditions an improved second attempt. Unlike inference-time reflection approaches, such as Reflexion, the framework optimizes self-review with policy gradients and internalizes improvements into the base policy via selective distillation, ensuring they persist across future episodes. A cross-episode memory keeps successful self-reviews for reuse when encountering similar tasks in future episodes during training. We evaluate SRRL against a standard RLVR baseline using the GRPO optimizer across two language models, Qwen 3-4B and OLMo-3- 7B, on GSM8K benchmark. SRRL consistently outperforms the RLVR in final reward performance and achieves greater learning efficiency by successfully transforming feedback into behavioral improvement.

</details>


### [89] [Federated Physics-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Distributed Stability Control in Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05553)
*Omar Al-Refai,Ibrahim Shahbaz,Adam Ali Husseinat,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦多智能体近端策略优化和物理邻域的智能电网暂态稳定控制框架，在IEEE 39节点系统上实现了100%稳定，控制时间减少72.4%，控制功率降低7-14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网的暂态稳定控制需要快速抑制发电机频率和转子角的偏差，以防止级联故障。传统方法难以在实时性要求下实现高效的分散式控制，因此需要一种能够协同多智能体学习并满足实时部署要求的控制方法。

Method: 提出FedPPO-PG框架，将暂态稳定控制建模为协作多智能体强化学习问题。每个发电机配备独立局部行动者，结合Kron简化导纳矩阵识别的两个最强耦合电气邻域的频率偏差。采用CTDE范式，通过中心化评论家指导优势估计，引导策略初始化阶段从经典分散控制器热启动所有行动者。部署时各行动者独立执行，无需中心协调器。

Result: 在IEEE 39节点基准系统上，针对五种训练和三种未见故障场景的24次试验中，FedPPO-PG实现了100%稳定率，平均稳定时间减少72.4%，相比集中式基线控制功率降低7-14倍，单智能体推理延迟满足IEEE/IEC 60255-118-1-2018实时报告要求。

Conclusion: FedPPO-PG框架成功实现了暂态稳定控制的高效分散式执行，在保证实时性的同时显著提升了控制性能，为智能电网的暂态稳定控制提供了一种可行的多智能体强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: Transient stability control in smart grids requires rapid post-fault damping of generator frequency and rotor angle deviations to prevent cascading failures. This paper proposes FedPPO-PG, a Federated Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization framework with Physics-Grounded neighborhoods, which reformulates transient stability control as a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning problem optimized directly against closed-loop stability objectives. Each generator hosts an independent local actor augmented with the frequency deviations of its two most strongly coupled electrical neighbors, identified from the post-fault Kron-reduced susceptance matrix. A guided policy initialization phase warm-starts all actors from the classical decentralized controller, while a centralized critic guides advantage estimation under the centralized training--decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm. Evaluated on a simulation of the IEEE 39-bus benchmark system across five training and three unseen fault contingencies, FedPPO-PG achieves 100% stabilization in all 24 trials, reduces mean stability time by 72.4%, and cuts the control power by 7-14 times compared to the centralized baseline. Each actor executes independently with no central coordinator at deployment, and the per-actor inference latency satisfies the IEEE/IEC 60255-118-1-2018 real-time reporting requirements.

</details>


### [90] [EquiFiLM: Charge-Conditioned Equivariant Force Fields via Feature-wise Linear Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05559)
*Samuel Sahel-Schackis,Ken-ichi Nomura,Aiichiro Nakano,Matthias F. Kling,Thomas Linker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EquiFiLM，一种轻量级扩展方法，通过FiLM模块为等变基础机器学习力场添加连续外部条件控制，仅需少量训练数据即可准确模拟充电等外场驱动的非平衡态过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础机器学习力场（如MACE-MP-0和UMA）虽然在广泛的化学空间内达到了接近DFT的精度，但它们假设平衡基态物理，无法原生处理外部诱导的电子态变化（如充电、外加场或电子激发），这限制了它们在光激发、电荷注入等驱动过程中的应用。

Method: 提出EquiFiLM方法，通过逐层的特征线性调制（FiLM）模块为任意等变基础MLFF添加连续外部条件控制。该模块仅调制标量通道，精确保持E(3)-等变性，能够从最少的训练数据中学习外场驱动的势能面变化。方法具有骨干网络无关和条件控制无关的特性。

Result: 在带电液态水体系上，以MACE-MatPES为骨干的E-MACE模型在四个训练电荷上实现了：力RMSE降低3.1倍（从21.3降至6.96 meV/Å），每原子能量RMSE降低61倍（从6.1降至0.1 meV/atom）。在七个测试电荷上，力RMSE保持在18-61 meV/Å范围内，能量RMSE保持在0.7-5.4 meV/atom范围内。模型在全测试范围内能稳定运行分子动力学模拟，并准确预测电荷依赖的第一壳层响应。相比从头训练需要约10^8个结构，该方法仅需几千帧DFT标注数据。

Conclusion: EquiFiLM提供了一种高效、通用的解决方案，能够以极低的训练成本扩展基础MLFF的适用范围至非平衡态驱动过程，为研究光激发、电荷注入等复杂物理化学过程提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Foundation machine learning force fields (MLFFs) such as MACE-MP-0 and UMA cover broad chemical space at near density functional theory (DFT) accuracy. However, they assume equilibrium ground-state physics and do not natively handle externally induced changes to the electronic state, such as charging, applied fields, or electronic excitation, which limits their use for driven processes such as photoexcitation and charge injection. We propose EquiFiLM, a lightweight extension that adds continuous external conditioning to any equivariant foundation MLFF via a per-layer Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) block, learning externally driven changes to the potential energy surface from minimal training data. The block modulates only scalar channels and preserves E(3)-equivariance exactly. We demonstrate the recipe on charged liquid water with the foundation model MACE-MatPES as the backbone, yielding E-MACE. On the four training charges, E-MACE delivers a $3.1\times$ reduction in force RMSE ($21.3$ to $6.96$ meV/$\mathring{A}$) and a $61\times$ reduction in per-atom energy RMSE ($6.1$ to $0.1$ meV/atom) over a baseline without EquiFiLM trained on the same data, at indistinguishable inference cost. Across seven held-out interpolation and extrapolation charges, force RMSE stays within $18-61$ meV/$\mathring{A}$ and energy RMSE within $0.7-5.4$ meV/atom. The model runs stable molecular dynamics across the full range tested and predicts the charge-dependent first-shell response of the reduced pair distribution function probed by ultrafast electron diffraction. Adding this conditioning axis to the foundation requires only a few thousand DFT-labeled frames, against the $\approx 10^8$ structures of a charge-aware foundation trained from scratch. The recipe is backbone- and conditioning-agnostic: it applies without architectural change to any equivariant MLFF with scalar interaction-layer channels.

</details>


### [91] [SafeImpute: Reliable Clinical Data Imputation via Conformal Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05613)
*Xinrui He,Mengting Ai,Junting Wang,Curtiss B. Cook,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeImpute框架，用于稀疏临床数据的可靠插补，在保证准确性的同时通过FDR控制机制筛选可靠的插补结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床护理依赖关键实验室指标，但真实世界患者就诊稀疏且检测不规律，导致数据大量缺失。现有插补方法虽提高平均精度，但无法判断哪些插补值足够可靠用于高风险下游任务。

Method: 构建事件图捕获患者内时间轨迹和患者间临床相似性，使用双关系GNN和自适应融合学习插补，通过辅助掩码重建目标正则化；将代理风险分数转换为共形p值，应用Benjamini-Hochberg过程控制不可接受错误的错误发现率(FDR)。

Result: 在Mayo Clinic数据集、公开MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上的实验表明，SafeImpute在标准插补评估和FDR控制的选择性发布评估中均优于多种基线方法，实现了强插补精度和可靠的错误控制。

Conclusion: SafeImpute成功实现了临床纵向记录的可靠插补，在保证插补准确性的同时提供了统计学上可证明的可靠性保证，为高风险临床应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Clinical care often relies on key laboratory indicators, yet real-world patient visits are sparse and tests are ordered irregularly, leading to pervasive missingness. While many imputation methods improve average accuracy, they provide limited guidance on which imputed values are reliable enough for high-stakes downstream use. In this work, we study reliable clinical imputation, aiming to produce accurate imputations while selectively releasing the reliable results, with statistical control over clinically unacceptable errors. To achieve this goal, we propose SafeImpute, a reliable imputation framework for irregular and sparse clinical longitudinal records. SafeImpute constructs an event graph that captures both intra-patient temporal trajectories and inter-patient clinical similarity, and learns imputations with a two-relation GNN and adaptive fusion, regularized by an auxiliary masked reconstruction objective. For reliability guarantees, SafeImpute converts a proxy risk score into conformal p-values and applies the Benjamini--Hochberg procedure to control the false discovery rate (FDR) of unacceptable errors among released imputations at a user-specified tolerance. Experiments on our Mayo Clinic data, the public MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets show that SafeImpute achieves strong imputation accuracy while providing reliable error control, outperforming diverse baselines in both standard imputation evaluation and FDR-controlled selective-release evaluation.

</details>


### [92] [A Coin Flip Per Token: Bernoulli Sparse Steering of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05615)
*Nima Eshraghi,Lovedeep Gondara,Yuqing Huang,Sagarika Suresh,Leizer Teran,Jithin Pradeep,Xiaotong Xu,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机Token/块干预策略，仅需干预30-50%的token即可达到或超越密集干预效果，同时保持流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE引导方法在每个生成token上施加信号，导致持续的干扰并可能损害模型流畅性，作者质疑这种密集干预是否必要。

Method: 提出两种无需奖励模型或学习门控策略的方法：随机Token引导(STS)以概率p独立对每个token进行门控；随机块引导(SBS)对序列中的前导窗口进行一次性门控。

Result: 在两个模型家族和两个行为任务上，仅干预50%的token即可恢复大部分密集干预效果并保持流畅性，干预30%的token即可超越基于提示的控制方法；最优引导幅度与干预比例成反比关系。

Conclusion: SAE介导的控制存在速率限制，行为效果取决于整个序列中的累积信号剂量，而非每个token的密集干预。

Abstract: Activation steering via sparse autoencoders (SAEs) enables behavioral control of large language models without task-specific fine-tuning, but standard methods apply the steering signal at every generated token, incurring constant per-token perturbation that risks degrading fluency. We ask: is dense intervention necessary? We introduce Stochastic Token Steering (STS), which gates each token independently with probability $p$, and Stochastic Block Steering (SBS), which gates a leading window once per sequence; neither requires a reward model or learned gating policy. Across two model families and two behavioral tasks, steering only 50% of the tokens recovers most of the dense-steering effect while preserving fluency, and steering as few as 30% surpasses prompt-based control. The optimal steering magnitude scales inversely with the intervention ratio, revealing that SAE-mediated control is rate-limited: the behavioral outcome depends on cumulative signal dosage across a sequence.

</details>


### [93] [Safe Bayesian Optimization with Counterfactual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05620)
*Katherine Avery,Bruno Castro da Silva,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种安全贝叶斯优化方法，通过共形预测估计反事实基准结果的不确定性区间，在仅能观测到基准策略反事实结果的情况下，确保以用户指定速率满足安全约束。


<details>
